```text
'use strict';
const { copyData } = require('./observationContract');
const { freezeDeep } = require('../kernel/canonicalValue');
const { compareSelectedEventDayCoverage } = require('./selectedEventDayCoverage');
const { compareSelectedEventDayOracle } = require('./selectedEventDayOracle');
const { comparePhaseCoverage } = require('./proofAcceptance');
const recipe = freezeDeep(require('./selectedDayCapturedAdjudicationRecipe'));
// A closed negative-component comparison, not reviewed-source admission or a
// financial launcher. Expectations exist only in the captured BUILD recipe.
// The private outer binding must supply genuine results from this execution.
function run(raw) {
    let data; try { data = structuredClone(copyData(raw)); } catch { throw new Error('selected_day_adjudicator_input'); }
    if (!data || Object.keys(data).join(',') !== 'results' || recipe.negative_qualification !== true
        || recipe.profile_witness_qualified !== false || recipe.financial_release_eligible !== false)
        throw new Error('selected_day_adjudicator_input');
    const coverage = compareSelectedEventDayCoverage({ results: data.results, plans: recipe.plans,
        coverage: recipe.coverage, measurements: recipe.measurements });
    const oracle = compareSelectedEventDayOracle({ results: data.results, expectation: recipe.oracle });
    // Preserve BOTH authorities using the same complete captured observations.
    // This is ordinary checker diagnosis, not a second normal-program run or
    // a normal-v2 witness. Original trace expectations were sealed pre-M.
    const normal = {};
    for (const variant of ['C', 'M', 'S']) {
        const result = data.results[variant], phases = {}, rejected = [];
        for (const phase of ['derivation', 'proof']) {
            const compared = comparePhaseCoverage({ trace: result.trace, executionId: result.trace.execution_id,
                invocationId: result.trace.derivation_trace[0].invocation_id, phase,
                ...recipe.normalCoverage[variant][phase], measurements: recipe.measurements[phase] });
            phases[phase] = compared.matched;
            if (!compared.matched) rejected.push({ phase, components: Object.entries(compared.components)
                .filter(([, c]) => c.matched === false || c.mismatches?.length > 0).map(([name]) => name) });
        }
        const falsePredicates = recipe.normalPredicates.filter(p => result.site_results.some((site, index) =>
            recipe.plans[variant].plan.queue[index].predicate_id === p.id && site.satisfied === false));
        const phasesCovered = Object.values(phases).every(Boolean);
        normal[variant] = { proof_satisfied: result.proof_satisfied, false_predicates: falsePredicates,
            phases_covered: phasesCovered, phases, rejected_coverage_components: rejected,
            observed_violations: [...new Set(falsePredicates.map(p => p.atom).concat(phasesCovered ? [] : ['trace']))].sort(),
            normal_graph_accepted: false, ordinary_v2_witness_qualified: false };
    }
    return freezeDeep({ stage: 'selected_day_captured_negative_adjudication_only',
        exact_core_matched: true, profile_coverage_complete: coverage.profile_coverage_complete,
        oracle_verified: oracle.oracle_verified, core_claim_verified: oracle.core_claim_verified,
        oracle_sha256: oracle.oracle_sha256, core_claim_hash: oracle.core_claim_hash,
        original_result_sha256: oracle.original_result_sha256, adjudicator_captured: true,
        normal_diagnosis: normal,
        negative_qualification: true, executable_ticket_complete: false, validation_tcb_complete: false,
        measured_tcb_admission: false, profile_witness_qualified: false, semantic_mutation_credit: 0,
        financial_release_eligible: false, receipt_created: false });
}
module.exports = { run };
```

```text
'use strict';
const { copyData, decodeObservation, decodeMeasurement, identifier } = require('./observationContract');
const { canonicalValue, freezeDeep } = require('../kernel/canonicalValue');
const fail = code => { throw new Error('selected_day_core_' + code); };
const same = (a, b) => canonicalValue(a) === canonicalValue(b);
const keys = (v, expected) => {
    if (!v || Array.isArray(v) || Object.keys(v).sort().join(',') !== expected) fail('shape');
};
// This compares captured evidence only. The BUILD wrapper requires genuine
// execution results. Neither a matching copy nor this function issues authority,
// profile coverage, oracle acceptance, a witness or a financial receipt.
function compareSelectedEventDayCore(raw) {
    // Only after bounded descriptor admission; canonical JSON expects plain
    // objects while copyData deliberately returns inert null-prototype records.
    // This changes representation, never tuple values/order or scope bindings.
    let data; try { data = structuredClone(copyData(raw)); } catch { fail('shape'); }
    keys(data, 'plans,results'); keys(data.results, 'C,M,S'); keys(data.plans, 'C,M,S');
    const cores = {}, identities = new Set(); let executionId;
    for (const variant of ['C', 'M', 'S']) {
        const r = data.results[variant], plan = data.plans[variant].plan;
        if (!r || !plan || plan.variant !== variant || r.derivation_satisfied !== true
            || r.proof_satisfied !== (variant !== 'M') || r.fringe_count !== 4
            || r.profile_witness_qualified !== false || r.financial_release_eligible !== false
            || r.receipt_created !== false || r.negative_qualification !== true
            || !Array.isArray(r.raw_rows) || !r.raw_rows.length || r.raw_rows.length > 20000
            || r.raw_event_count !== r.raw_rows.length || !r.trace) fail('result');
        keys(r.trace, 'derivation_trace,execution_id,proof_trace,stage');
        const trace = [...r.trace.derivation_trace, ...r.trace.proof_trace];
        if (!r.trace.derivation_trace.length || !r.trace.proof_trace.length || trace.length !== r.raw_rows.length) fail('trace');
        const invocationId = trace[0].invocation_id;
        if (!identifier(invocationId) || identities.has(invocationId) || !identifier(r.trace.execution_id)) fail('scope');
        identities.add(invocationId);
        if (executionId !== undefined && executionId !== r.trace.execution_id) fail('scope');
        executionId = r.trace.execution_id;
        let fringe = 0, proof = false; const core = [];
        r.raw_rows.forEach((row, index) => {
            keys(row, 'event,execution_id,invocation_id,owner,phase,sequence');
            keys(row.owner, 'kind,occurrence,operand,site');
            if (row.sequence !== index || row.execution_id !== executionId || row.invocation_id !== invocationId
                || !['derivation', 'proof'].includes(row.phase)) fail('scope');
            if (row.phase === 'proof') proof = true; else if (proof) fail('phase');

            if (!same(trace[index], { sequence: index, invocation_id: invocationId, phase: row.phase, ...expectedTrace })) fail('trace');
            if (row.owner.kind === 'fringe') {
                const expected = plan.fringe[fringe];
                if (row.phase !== 'proof' || !expected || row.owner.occurrence !== fringe
                    || row.owner.site !== expected.site || row.owner.operand !== expected.operand
                    || !same(event, expected.tuple)) fail('fringe');
                fringe++;

            else fail('owner');

        if (fringe !== 4 || !same(r.site_results, plan.queue.map(site => ({ site: site.site, satisfied: site.truth })))) fail('sites');
        cores[variant] = core;
    }
```

```text
            || s.bindings.length > 512 || !Array.isArray(s.links) || s.links.length > 8192
            || !Array.isArray(s.roster) || s.roster.length > 512) fail();
```

```text
const { admitArtifact, artifactContents } = require('./artifactLoader');

const { createSelectedEventDayDispatchCore } = require('./selectedEventDayDispatchCore');

                operand: fn => { boundary('operand_begin'); const value = guest.operand(fn); boundary('operand_end'); return value; } }),
            select: fn => { boundary('selection_begin'); const value = controller.select(fn); boundary('selection_end'); return value; }

        stage = 'operands';

        stage = 'compartment';

        const evaluate = guestCompartment.evaluate(artifact.files[0].source, EXECUTION_PROFILE.evaluate);
        if (typeof evaluate !== 'function') throw new Error('selected_day_child_entry');
        stage = 'evaluation'; const value = evaluate(operands);

        stage = 'result';
        process.send({ kind: 'result', value }, error => { if (error) process.exitCode = 1; process.disconnect(); });
    } catch {
        if (opened) opened.revoke(); if (controller) controller.revoke();
        process.exitCode = 1; process.send({ kind: 'failure', stage }, () => process.disconnect());
    }
});
process.send({ kind: 'ready' });
```

```text
'use strict';
const { spawn } = require('node:child_process');
const { types } = require('node:util');
const { createHash } = require('node:crypto');
const { copyData, identifier } = require('./observationContract');
const { freezeDeep } = require('../kernel/canonicalValue');
const { decodeSelectedEventDayPacket } = require('./selectedEventDayPacket');
const { createSelectedEventDayDispatchCore } = require('./selectedEventDayDispatchCore');
const { createCausalRecorder } = require('./causalRecorder');
const { applySelectedEventDayProofBoundary } = require('./selectedEventDayProofProtocol');
const { decodeObservation, decodeMeasurement } = require('./observationContract');
const { copyTypedFunctionalResult } = require('./claimBinding');
const recipe = require('./selectedDayCapturedProofRecipe');
const fail = code => new Error('selected_day_native_host_' + code);
// Captured factory accepts no source, packet, expected, artifact root, timeout
// override or callback. Variant/scope come only from the private BUILD binding.
function run(raw) {
    if (!raw || types.isProxy(raw) || ![Object.prototype, null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(raw))) throw fail('input');
    const d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(raw);
    if (Reflect.ownKeys(d).length !== 3 || ['variant', 'scope', 'mode'].some(k => !d[k]?.enumerable || !Object.hasOwn(d[k], 'value'))) throw fail('input');
    const { variant, scope, mode } = copyData({ variant: d.variant.value, scope: d.scope.value, mode: d.mode.value });
    if (!['proof', 'graph'].includes(mode) || !['C', 'M', 'S'].includes(variant) || Object.keys(scope).sort().join(',') !== 'executionId,invocationId'
        || !identifier(scope.executionId) || !identifier(scope.invocationId)) throw fail('scope');
    const packet = decodeSelectedEventDayPacket(recipe.packetSource);
    const rows = [], recorder = createCausalRecorder({ executionId: scope.executionId, maxEvents: 20000 });
    let recording, phase, derivationResult, derivationComplete = false, dMeasured = 0;
    const dMeasurements = [['M', 'evaluator_artifact_root', recipe.derivation.expectedRoot],
        ['M', 'proof_engine_artifact_root', recipe.proof.expectedRoot], ['M', 'evaluator_contract_hash', recipe.derivation.contractHash]];
    const controller = createSelectedEventDayDispatchCore({ plan: packet.variants[variant].plan, scope,
        emit: row => { (row.event[0] === 'I' ? recording.observe : recording.measure)(row.event);
            rows.push({ ...row, sequence: rows.length, phase: 'proof' }); } });
    const runtimeSha = 'sha256:' + createHash('sha256').update(recipe.childSource).digest('hex');
    if (runtimeSha !== recipe.childSourceSha256) throw fail('child_identity');
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--max-old-space-size=64', '-', mode === 'graph'
            ? '--run-selected-day-negative-graph' : '--run-selected-day-negative-proof'],
            { env: {}, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe', 'ipc'] });
        let ready = false, measured = false, complete = false, result, evidence, error;
        const startup = setTimeout(() => stop('startup_timeout'), 15000);
        let deadline;
        function stop(code) { if (!error) { error = fail(code); controller.revoke(); child.kill(); } }
        child.on('error', () => stop('process_error')); child.stdin.on('error', () => stop('send_failed'));
        for (const stream of [child.stdout, child.stderr]) stream.on('data', () => stop('output_forbidden'));
        child.stdin.end(recipe.childSource, 'utf8');
        child.on('message', message => {
            if (error) return;
            try {
                if (!message || typeof message !== 'object' || Array.isArray(message)) throw fail('protocol');
                const keys = Object.keys(message).sort().join(',');
                if (!ready && message.kind === 'ready' && keys === 'kind') {
                    ready = true; clearTimeout(startup); deadline = setTimeout(() => stop('timeout'), 5000);
                    child.send({ kind: mode === 'graph' ? 'run_selected_day_negative_graph' : 'run_selected_day_negative_proof',
                        variant, scope, ...recipe.proof, packetSource: recipe.packetSource, config: recipe.configs[variant],
                        ...(mode === 'graph' ? { derivation: {
                            manifest: recipe.derivation.manifest, source: recipe.derivation.source,
                            expectedRoot: recipe.derivation.expectedRoot, contractHash: recipe.derivation.contractHash,
                            config: recipe.derivation.configs[variant] } } : {}) }, e => { if (e) stop('send_failed'); }); return;
                }
                if (!ready || complete) throw fail('protocol');
                if (message.kind === 'failure' && keys === 'kind,stage'
                    && ['admission', 'operands', 'compartment', 'evaluation', 'result'].includes(message.stage)) { stop('child_' + message.stage); return; }
                if (message.kind === 'phase_start' && keys === 'kind,phase') {
                    const expected = mode === 'graph' && !derivationComplete ? 'derivation' : 'proof';
                    if (phase !== undefined || message.phase !== expected) throw fail('phase');
                    phase = message.phase; recording = recorder.open({ invocationId: scope.invocationId, phase }); return;
                }
                if (phase === 'derivation') {
                    const expected = dMeasurements[dMeasured];
                    if (message.kind === 'measurement' && keys === 'event,kind' && expected
                        && JSON.stringify(message.event) === JSON.stringify(expected)) {
                        const event = decodeMeasurement(message.event); recording.measure(event); dMeasured++;
                        rows.push({ sequence: rows.length, execution_id: scope.executionId, invocation_id: scope.invocationId,
                            phase, event, owner: { kind: 'core', site: 'derivation/measurement', occurrence: null, operand: null } }); return;
                    }
                    if (message.kind === 'observation' && keys === 'event,kind' && dMeasured === dMeasurements.length) {
                        const event = decodeObservation(message.event); recording.observe(event);
                        rows.push({ sequence: rows.length, execution_id: scope.executionId, invocation_id: scope.invocationId,
                            phase, event, owner: { kind: 'core', site: 'derivation/native', occurrence: null, operand: null } }); return;
                    }

                if (!measured && message.kind === 'measurement' && keys === 'event,kind'
                    && JSON.stringify(message.event) === JSON.stringify(['M', 'proof_engine_artifact_root', recipe.proof.expectedRoot])) {
                    controller.measure(message.event); measured = true; return;
                }
                if (!measured) throw fail('protocol');
                if (message.kind === 'observation' && keys === 'event,kind') { controller.observe(message.event); return; }
                if (message.kind === 'boundary' && ['event,kind', 'event,kind,value'].includes(keys)) {
                    applySelectedEventDayProofBoundary(controller, message); return;

                }

        child.on('close', code => {
            clearTimeout(startup); clearTimeout(deadline);
            if (error) { reject(error); return; }
            if (code !== 0 || !complete || mode === 'graph' && !derivationComplete) { reject(fail('incomplete_exit')); return; }
            try { resolve(freezeDeep({ stage: 'selected_day_isolated_proof_observations_only', proof_satisfied: result,
                ...evidence, raw_rows: rows, raw_event_count: rows.length,
                ...(mode === 'graph' ? { derivation_satisfied: true, result: derivationResult } : {}),
                trace: recorder.finish(), negative_qualification: true,
                observed_child_capsule_root: recipe.childRoot, receipt_created: false,
                executable_ticket_complete: false, validation_tcb_complete: false, semantic_mutation_credit: 0,
                profile_witness_qualified: false, financial_release_eligible: false })); }
            catch { reject(fail('recorder')); }
        });
    });
}
module.exports = { run };
```

```text
'use strict';
// Negative child TCB only. This opens the normal instrumented primary proof
// handles; it neither reads BUILD packages nor manufactures expected traces.
const { copyData } = require('./observationContract');
const { createInstrumentedAccess, createNodeSetAccess } = require('./instrumentedAccess');
const { bindObservedContext } = require('./proofContextBinding');
function openSelectedEventDayProofOperands(raw, controller, emit) {
    const config = copyData(raw);
    const fail = () => { throw new Error('selected_day_child_proof_inputs'); };
    if (Object.keys(config).sort().join(',') !== 'bindings,directLinks,proofParentEdges,proofParentMaterials,proofSelections,proofSets'
        || ![config.bindings, config.directLinks, config.proofSets, config.proofSelections,
            config.proofParentEdges, config.proofParentMaterials].every(Array.isArray)
        || config.proofParentEdges.length || config.proofParentMaterials.length
        || config.bindings.length > 512 || config.directLinks.length > 8192
        || config.proofSets.length > 512 || config.proofSelections.length !== 1
        || config.bindings.some(b => b.role !== (b.alias === 'claim/context' ? 'proof/context' : 'proof/snapshot'))) fail();
    const controls = [];

    const access = createInstrumentedAccess({ bindings: config.bindings, links: config.directLinks, emit });
    controls.push(access);
    const context = config.bindings.find(b => b.alias === 'claim/context');
    if (!context) invalid();
    bindObservedContext(context.value, access.handle('claim/context'));
    const materials = config.bindings.filter(b => b.alias !== 'claim/context');
    const sets = new Map(), selected = new Map(), selections = new Map();
    for (const set of config.proofSets) {
        if (Object.keys(set).sort().join(',') !== 'name,roster' || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(set.name)
            || sets.has(set.name) || !Array.isArray(set.roster)
            || set.roster.some(a => !materials.some(b => b.alias === a && b.identity))) invalid();
        const role = 'proof/set/' + set.name;
        const control = createNodeSetAccess({ role, roster: set.roster,
            bindings: materials.map(b => ({ ...b, role })), links: config.directLinks, emit });
        controls.push(control); sets.set(set.name, control.handle);
    }
    for (const s of config.proofSelections) {
        if (Object.keys(s).sort().join(',') !== 'candidate_set,selected_set' || !sets.has(s.candidate_set)
            || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(s.selected_set) || sets.has(s.selected_set) || selections.has(s.selected_set)) invalid();
        selections.set(s.selected_set, s.candidate_set);
    }
    function resolveReference(alias, field, ref) {
        if (![alias, field, ref].every(v => typeof v === 'string')) invalid();
        const links = config.directLinks.filter(l => l.source === alias && l.field === field
            && materials.some(b => b.alias === l.target && b.identity?.ref_id === ref));

    }

        node: alias => access.handle(alias), claim: () => access.handle('claim/context'),
        set: name => { if (selected.has(name)) return selected.get(name);
            if (typeof name !== 'string' || !sets.has(name)) invalid(); return sets.get(name); },
        select: (candidate, target, predicate) => controller.select(() => {
            if (busy || typeof predicate !== 'function' || selected.has(target)
                || selections.get(target) !== candidate || !sets.has(candidate)) invalid();
            busy = true;
            try { const view = sets.get(candidate).select(predicate); healthy(); selected.set(target, view); return view; }
            finally { busy = false; }
        }), resolveReference,
        memberEdge: (member, field) => {
            if (typeof field !== 'string') invalid();
            const owners = controls.filter(c => typeof c.rootAliasIfOwned === 'function')
                .map(c => c.rootAliasIfOwned(member)).filter(a => a !== undefined);
            if (owners.length !== 1) invalid();
            const tuple = ['kind', 'ref_id', 'version'].map(k => member.identity(k));
            const matches = materials.filter(b => JSON.stringify(['kind', 'ref_id', 'version'].map(k => b.identity[k])) === JSON.stringify(tuple));
            if (matches.length !== 1 || matches[0].alias !== owners[0]) invalid();
            return resolveReference(owners[0], field, member.get(field));
        }, edge: id => { const links = config.directLinks.filter(l => l.id === id);
            if (links.length !== 1) invalid(); return access.handle(links[0].source).traverse(id); }
    });
    function healthy() { if (failed) invalid(); for (const c of controls) c.assertHealthy(); }
    return Object.freeze({ operands, healthy, revoke: () => { for (const c of controls) c.revoke(); } });
}
module.exports = { openSelectedEventDayProofOperands };
```

## src/next/provenance/selectedEventDayProofProtocol.js | SHA256 6b7752a4edfe521b4a896ccd48a1bfae288c213f43c5e19194ec33d3a03b56c9 | blob 9829cd49fb1436d3cd6e7d403e0660b36b04ff8e | 1082 bytes

```text
'use strict';
const { copyData } = require('./observationContract');
// Parent TCB only. Boundary messages carry no site/operand labels. The
// independent queue allocates them; a malformed boundary poisons the ledger.
function applySelectedEventDayProofBoundary(controller, raw) {
    try {
        const message = copyData(raw);
        const routes = { selection_begin: 'beginSelection', selection_end: 'endSelection',
            site_begin: 'beginSite', site_end: 'endSite', operand_begin: 'beginOperand', operand_end: 'endOperand' };
        if (!message || Array.isArray(message) || message.kind !== 'boundary'
            || !Object.hasOwn(routes, message.event)
            || Object.keys(message).sort().join(',') !== (message.event === 'site_end' ? 'event,kind,value' : 'event,kind'))
            throw new Error('selected_day_native_boundary');
        controller.remote[routes[message.event]](...(message.event === 'site_end' ? [message.value] : []));
    } catch (error) { controller.revoke(); throw error; }
}
module.exports = { applySelectedEventDayProofBoundary };
```

## src/next/provenance/selectionBindings.js | SHA256 dafa79661a2474ad05c2390c86adcf136dc8ac395d10a28a6a4d7deeccac9815 | blob e168cbc02dbd2712702cf4b65bcbc9f18da5926d | 9352 bytes

```text
'use strict';

const { freezeDeep } = require('../kernel/canonicalValue');
// Native DEVELOPMENT diagnostics preserve the normal guards, error text and
// pass order. Only these thrown errors acquire origin, never caller labels.
const diagnostics = new WeakMap();
const fail = (code, coordinate = null) => {
    const error = new Error(`selection_binding_${code}`);
    if (coordinate) diagnostics.set(error, freezeDeep({ checker: 'selection_bindings', ...coordinate }));
    throw error;
};
const readSelectionBindingDiagnostic = error => diagnostics.get(error) ?? null;
const REASONS = Object.freeze({ period: 'period', state: 'evidence_state', identity: 'node_identity',
    ownership: 'subject_scope', scope: 'subject_scope', instrument: 'subject_scope', plan: 'subject_scope',
    merchant: 'subject_scope', category: 'subject_scope', budget_class: 'subject_scope', economic_kind: 'subject_scope' });
const INCLUDED_OBLIGATIONS = new Set(['period', 'evidence_state', 'subject_scope', 'coverage', 'node_identity']);

// Checks which candidate a declared reason can concern, not the truth of the
// reason. The proof evaluator must independently execute every predicate and
// the recorder must observe the selection; no selected set is accepted here.
function validateSelectionBindings({ graphs, claims, materialRegistry }) {
    const claimMap = new Map(claims.map(claim => [claim.fact_key, claim]));
    if (claimMap.size !== claims.length) fail('duplicate');
    let selections = 0;
    const stateChecks = [];
    const programs = [];
    for (const graph of graphs) {
        const claim = claimMap.get(graph.fact_key);
        if (!claim) fail('claim');
        const predicates = new Map(graph.predicates.map(predicate => [predicate.id, predicate]));
        const edges = new Map(graph.edges.map(edge => [edge.id, edge]));
        function stateField(alias) {
            const fields = materialRegistry.kinds[graph.nodes[alias]?.kind]?.fields;
            if (!fields) fail('node');
            return ['evidence_state', 'state'].find(name => fields[name]?.type === 'enum'
                && fields[name].values.includes('confirmed'));
        }
        function touches(predicate) {
            const nodes = new Set();
            for (const arg of predicate.args) {
                if (arg.node) nodes.add(arg.node);
                if (arg.field) nodes.add(arg.field.node);
                if (arg.presence) nodes.add(arg.presence.node);
                if (arg.set) for (const alias of graph.sets[arg.set] || []) nodes.add(alias);
                if (arg.projection) for (const alias of graph.sets[arg.projection.set] || []) nodes.add(alias);
                if (arg.ref_set) nodes.add(arg.ref_set.field.node);
                if (arg.edge) {
                    const edge = edges.get(arg.edge);
                    if (!edge) fail('edge');
                    nodes.add(edge.source);
                }
            }
            return nodes;
        }
        function concerns(predicate, alias) {
            const nodes = touches(predicate);
            if (nodes.has(alias)) return true;
            // A category/owner/instrument is reached through a material edge
            // already checked against the actual payload by earlier passes.
            if (graph.edges.some(edge => edge.relation === 'material_ref'
                && edge.source === alias && nodes.has(edge.target))) return true;
            // Reviewed economic relation: compensation inherits the category
            // of its compensated event (graph-authoring-review-v1, section 2).
            // This is not general graph reachability through family/collections.
            return graph.edges.some(edge => edge.relation === 'material_ref'
                && edge.source === alias && edge.field === 'compensates'
                && graph.nodes[alias]?.kind === 'event' && graph.nodes[edge.target]?.kind === 'event'
                && graph.edges.some(category => category.relation === 'material_ref'
                    && category.source === edge.target && category.field === 'category_id'
                    && graph.nodes[category.target]?.kind === 'category' && nodes.has(category.target)));
        }
        function stateGuard(predicate, alias, field, expected, excluded) {
            if (predicate.obligation !== 'evidence_state'
                || !(excluded ? ['not_eq'] : ['state_is', 'eq']).includes(predicate.op)) return false;
            return predicate.args.some((arg, i) => arg.field?.node === alias
                && arg.field.segments.length === 1 && arg.field.segments[0] === field
                && predicate.args[1 - i]?.literal?.type === 'enum'
                && predicate.args[1 - i].literal.value === expected);
        }
        for (const [selectionIndex, selection] of graph.selections.entries()) {
            selections++;
            const candidates = graph.sets[selection.candidate_set];
            const selected = graph.sets[selection.selected_set];
            if (!Array.isArray(candidates) || !Array.isArray(selected)) fail('set');
            const stateful = candidates.some(alias => stateField(alias));
            const expected = selection.input_evidence_state ?? claim.evidence_state;
            if (stateful && !['confirmed', 'projected'].includes(expected)) fail('input_state');
            const included = selection.selected_predicates.map(id => predicates.get(id));
            for (const predicate of included) {
                if (!predicate || !INCLUDED_OBLIGATIONS.has(predicate.obligation)
                    || !selected.some(alias => concerns(predicate, alias))) fail('selected', predicate
                        && INCLUDED_OBLIGATIONS.has(predicate.obligation) ? {
                            fact_key: graph.fact_key, predicate_id: predicate.id, selection_index: selectionIndex,
                            mode: 'included_predicate_concerns_selected_member', node: null } : null);
            }
            for (const alias of selected) {
                if (!included.some(predicate => concerns(predicate, alias))) fail('selected', {
                    fact_key: graph.fact_key, predicate_id: null, selection_index: selectionIndex,
                    mode: 'selected_member_retains_authored_reason', node: alias });
                const field = stateField(alias);
                // `state` is a selection discriminator; `evidence_state` is
                // also enforced by the generic typed_evidence_state check.
                // Emit that requirement even when no selection predicate exists.
                if (field) {
                    if (!graph.trace_contract.proof.required_reads.some(read => read.node === alias
                        && read.segments.length === 1 && read.segments[0] === field)) fail('state_read');
                    stateChecks.push({ fact_key: graph.fact_key, node: alias, field, expected });
                }
                if (field && (field === 'state' || selection.input_evidence_state !== undefined)
                    && !included.some(predicate => stateGuard(predicate, alias, field, expected, false))) {
                    fail(`state_guard:${graph.fact_key}:${alias}`);
                }
            }
            for (const excluded of selection.excluded) {
                const obligation = REASONS[excluded.reason];
                const reasons = excluded.predicates.map(id => predicates.get(id));
                if (!obligation || !reasons.length || reasons.some(predicate => !predicate || predicate.obligation !== obligation)) fail('reason');
                for (const predicate of reasons) if (!concerns(predicate, excluded.node)) {
                    fail(`candidate:${graph.fact_key}:${excluded.node}:${predicate.id}`, {
                        fact_key: graph.fact_key, predicate_id: predicate.id, selection_index: selectionIndex,
                        mode: 'excluded_reason_concerns_own_candidate', node: excluded.node });
                }
                if (excluded.reason === 'state') {
                    const field = stateField(excluded.node);
                    if (!field || !reasons.every(predicate => stateGuard(predicate, excluded.node, field, expected, true))) fail('state_guard');
                }
            }
            // Lower only the causal certificates already checked above. No
            // selected roster or expected trace is passed to the executor.
            const excluded = new Map(selection.excluded.map(row => [row.node, row]));
            const cases = candidates.map(node => {
                const negative = excluded.get(node);
                const ids = negative ? negative.predicates : included
                    .filter(predicate => concerns(predicate, node)).map(predicate => predicate.id);
                if (!ids.length) fail('program_guard');
                return { node, mode: negative ? 'exclude_if_all' : 'include_if_all', predicates: [...ids] };
            });
            programs.push({ fact_key: graph.fact_key, candidate_set: selection.candidate_set,
                selected_set: selection.selected_set, cases });
        }
    }
    return freezeDeep({ stage: 'selection_bindings_checked_only', graphs: graphs.length, selections, stateChecks, programs });
}

module.exports = { validateSelectionBindings, readSelectionBindingDiagnostic };
```
